{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01VfvfCbWi6bKkL7xBbx9fik","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01CsiXuMuc3eG3bvsPL51pxh","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westb-48145","command":"mkdir -p /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion && cat > /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion/run_download_eval_pure.sh << 'SCRIPT'\n#!/bin/bash\nset -eo pipefail\nsource /etc/network_turbo\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\nexport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\nexport HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1\nexport REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\nexport SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\nexport HF_HUB_ENABLE_HF_TRANSFER=1\n\nMODELS=\"/root/entrainment_gated_ft/models\"\nRESULTS=\"/root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion\"\nLOG=\"$RESULTS/pure_download_eval.log\"\n\necho \"=== PURE DOWNLOAD+EVAL START $(date) ===\" | tee \"$LOG\"\n\n# ---- DOWNLOAD ----\ndeclare -A REPOS\nREPOS[GLA]=\"m-a-p/340M-20B-GLA-pure-baseline\"\nREPOS[DeltaNet]=\"m-a-p/340M-20B-DeltaNet-pure\"\nREPOS[HGRN]=\"m-a-p/340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline\"\n\ndeclare -A LOCAL_DIRS\nLOCAL_DIRS[GLA]=\"$MODELS/340M-20B-GLA-pure-baseline\"\nLOCAL_DIRS[DeltaNet]=\"$MODELS/340M-20B-DeltaNet-pure\"\nLOCAL_DIRS[HGRN]=\"$MODELS/340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline\"\n\nfor ARCH in GLA DeltaNet HGRN; do\n  REPO=\"${REPOS[$ARCH]}\"\n  LOCAL=\"${LOCAL_DIRS[$ARCH]}\"\n  \n  if [ -f \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\" ]; then\n    echo \"$ARCH pure already exists at $LOCAL, skipping download\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n    continue\n  fi\n  \n  echo \"DOWNLOAD $ARCH pure from $REPO START $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  python3 -c \"\nimport os\nos.environ['HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET']='1'\nos.environ['HF_HUB_ENABLE_HF_TRANSFER']='1'\nfrom huggingface_hub import snapshot_download\nsnapshot_download('$REPO', local_dir='$LOCAL')\nprint('Download OK: $REPO')\n\" 2>&1 | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  echo \"DOWNLOAD $ARCH pure DONE $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\ndone\n\n# ---- VERIFY DOWNLOADS ----\necho \"=== VERIFY DOWNLOADS ===\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\nALL_OK=true\nfor ARCH in GLA DeltaNet HGRN; do\n  LOCAL=\"${LOCAL_DIRS[$ARCH]}\"\n  if [ -f \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\" ]; then\n    SIZE=$(stat -c%s \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\")\n    echo \"$ARCH pure: OK ($SIZE bytes)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  else\n    echo \"$ARCH pure: MISSING\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n    ALL_OK=false\n  fi\ndone\n\nif [ \"$ALL_OK\" = false ]; then\n  echo \"ERROR: Some downloads failed, aborting eval\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# ---- EVALUATE ----\necho \"=== PURE EVAL START $(date) ===\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n\nfor ARCH in GLA DeltaNet HGRN; do\n  LOCAL=\"${LOCAL_DIRS[$ARCH]}\"\n  OUTDIR=\"$RESULTS/${ARCH}_pure\"\n  \n  echo \"EVAL $ARCH pure START $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  python -m lm_eval \\\n    --model hf \\\n    --model_args pretrained=$LOCAL,trust_remote_code=True \\\n    --tasks lambada_openai,arc_easy \\\n    --device cuda:0 \\\n    --batch_size auto \\\n    --log_samples \\\n    --output_path \"$OUTDIR/\" 2>&1 | tail -20 | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  echo \"EVAL $ARCH pure DONE $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\ndone\n\necho \"=== ALL PURE DOWNLOAD+EVAL DONE $(date) ===\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\nSCRIPT\nchmod +x /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion/run_download_eval_pure.sh\necho \"Script written OK\"","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":889,"cache_read_input_tokens":33414,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":889},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f91c7e3f-c021-4ec0-8958-c2b588a62e76","uuid":"e1a804fc-656d-4997-a2f0-114f452cdb4b"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_017zbrLsEtKsExkcQRkothQ5","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EYrbvB1h2mxCwUd2dJ2r15","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westb-48145","command":"source /etc/network_turbo && rm -rf /root/entrainment_gated_ft/models/340M-20B-GLA-pure-baseline/.cache && cat > /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion/run_wget_eval_pure.sh << 'SCRIPT'\n#!/bin/bash\nset -eo pipefail\nsource /etc/network_turbo\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\nexport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\nexport HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1\nexport REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\nexport SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\n\nMODELS=\"/root/entrainment_gated_ft/models\"\nRESULTS=\"/root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion\"\nLOG=\"$RESULTS/pure_wget_eval.log\"\nFILES=\"config.json generation_config.json tokenizer.json tokenizer_config.json special_tokens_map.json model.safetensors\"\n\necho \"=== PURE WGET+EVAL START $(date) ===\" | tee \"$LOG\"\n\n# ---- DOWNLOAD via wget ----\ndeclare -A REPOS\nREPOS[GLA]=\"340M-20B-GLA-pure-baseline\"\nREPOS[DeltaNet]=\"340M-20B-DeltaNet-pure\"\nREPOS[HGRN]=\"340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline\"\n\nfor ARCH in GLA DeltaNet HGRN; do\n  REPO_NAME=\"${REPOS[$ARCH]}\"\n  LOCAL=\"$MODELS/$REPO_NAME\"\n  \n  if [ -f \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\" ]; then\n    SIZE=$(stat -c%s \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\")\n    if [ \"$SIZE\" -gt 100000000 ]; then\n      echo \"$ARCH: already exists ($SIZE bytes), skip\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n      continue\n    fi\n  fi\n  \n  mkdir -p \"$LOCAL\"\n  echo \"DOWNLOAD $ARCH ($REPO_NAME) START $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  \n  for f in $FILES; do\n    if [ -f \"$LOCAL/$f\" ] && [ \"$f\" != \"model.safetensors\" ]; then\n      echo \"  $f already exists, skip\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n      continue\n    fi\n    echo \"  Downloading $f...\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n    wget --timeout=300 --tries=5 --continue --progress=dot:mega \\\n      \"https://huggingface.co/m-a-p/$REPO_NAME/resolve/main/$f\" \\\n      -O \"$LOCAL/$f\" 2>&1 | tail -5 | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  done\n  \n  if [ -f \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\" ]; then\n    SIZE=$(stat -c%s \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\")\n    echo \"DOWNLOAD $ARCH DONE: model.safetensors=$SIZE bytes $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  else\n    echo \"DOWNLOAD $ARCH FAILED $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n    exit 1\n  fi\ndone\n\necho \"=== ALL DOWNLOADS DONE $(date) ===\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n\n# ---- EVALUATE ----\nfor ARCH in GLA DeltaNet HGRN; do\n  REPO_NAME=\"${REPOS[$ARCH]}\"\n  LOCAL=\"$MODELS/$REPO_NAME\"\n  OUTDIR=\"$RESULTS/${ARCH}_pure\"\n  \n  echo \"EVAL $ARCH pure START $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  python -m lm_eval \\\n    --model hf \\\n    --model_args pretrained=$LOCAL,trust_remote_code=True \\\n    --tasks lambada_openai,arc_easy \\\n    --device cuda:0 \\\n    --batch_size auto \\\n    --log_samples \\\n    --output_path \"$OUTDIR/\" 2>&1 | tail -20 | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  echo \"EVAL $ARCH pure DONE $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\ndone\n\necho \"=== ALL PURE WGET+EVAL DONE $(date) ===\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\nSCRIPT\nchmod +x /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion/run_wget_eval_pure.sh\necho \"Script written OK\"","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":232,"cache_read_input_tokens":47040,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":232},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f91c7e3f-c021-4ec0-8958-c2b588a62e76","uuid":"3dae487a-27ae-4d6d-ae75-816053448dd4"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01TnsekAL8e9xTBGTHfQsCwQ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"DeltaNet download is at 6.5MB and progressing at about 40-50KB/s. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_016e2GJAczEL9N7zHg5Xep14","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01VUW3Cuf296LKp7jTCb2BqC","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westb-48145","command":"cat > /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion/run_pure_v3.sh << 'SCRIPT'\n#!/bin/bash\nset -eo pipefail\nsource /etc/network_turbo\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\nexport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\nexport HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1\nexport HF_XET_HIGH_PERFORMANCE=0\nexport REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\nexport SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\n\nMODELS=\"/root/entrainment_gated_ft/models\"\nRESULTS=\"/root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion\"\nLOG=\"$RESULTS/pure_v3.log\"\n\necho \"=== PURE V3 START $(date) ===\" | tee \"$LOG\"\n\n# ---- VERIFY GLA ----\nif [ -f \"$MODELS/340M-20B-GLA-pure-baseline/model.safetensors\" ]; then\n  SIZE=$(stat -c%s \"$MODELS/340M-20B-GLA-pure-baseline/model.safetensors\")\n  echo \"GLA pure: already exists ($SIZE bytes)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\nelse\n  echo \"ERROR: GLA pure missing!\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# ---- DOWNLOAD DeltaNet + HGRN via Python (Xet disabled) ----\nfor ARCH_INFO in \"DeltaNet:m-a-p/340M-20B-DeltaNet-pure:340M-20B-DeltaNet-pure\" \"HGRN:m-a-p/340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline:340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline\"; do\n  IFS=':' read -r ARCH REPO LOCAL_NAME <<< \"$ARCH_INFO\"\n  LOCAL=\"$MODELS/$LOCAL_NAME\"\n\n  if [ -f \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\" ]; then\n    SIZE=$(stat -c%s \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\")\n    if [ \"$SIZE\" -gt 100000000 ]; then\n      echo \"$ARCH pure: already exists ($SIZE bytes), skip\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n      continue\n    fi\n  fi\n\n  echo \"DOWNLOAD $ARCH ($REPO) via Python START $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  python3 -c \"\nimport os\nos.environ['HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET'] = '1'\nos.environ['HF_XET_HIGH_PERFORMANCE'] = '0'\nfrom huggingface_hub import snapshot_download\nsnapshot_download(\n    '$REPO',\n    local_dir='$LOCAL',\n    force_download=True,\n)\nprint('Download OK: $REPO')\n\" 2>&1 | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  echo \"DOWNLOAD $ARCH DONE $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\ndone\n\n# ---- VERIFY ----\necho \"=== VERIFY ===\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\nALL_OK=true\ndeclare -A DIRS\nDIRS[GLA]=\"$MODELS/340M-20B-GLA-pure-baseline\"\nDIRS[DeltaNet]=\"$MODELS/340M-20B-DeltaNet-pure\"\nDIRS[HGRN]=\"$MODELS/340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline\"\n\nfor ARCH in GLA DeltaNet HGRN; do\n  LOCAL=\"${DIRS[$ARCH]}\"\n  if [ -f \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\" ]; then\n    SIZE=$(stat -c%s \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\")\n    echo \"$ARCH pure: OK ($SIZE bytes)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  else\n    echo \"$ARCH pure: MISSING\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n    ALL_OK=false\n  fi\ndone\n\nif [ \"$ALL_OK\" = false ]; then\n  echo \"ERROR: Some downloads failed\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# ---- EVALUATE ALL 3 ----\necho \"=== EVAL START $(date) ===\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\nfor ARCH in GLA DeltaNet HGRN; do\n  LOCAL=\"${DIRS[$ARCH]}\"\n  OUTDIR=\"$RESULTS/${ARCH}_pure\"\n  echo \"EVAL $ARCH pure START $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  python -m lm_eval \\\n    --model hf \\\n    --model_args pretrained=$LOCAL,trust_remote_code=True \\\n    --tasks lambada_openai,arc_easy \\\n    --device cuda:0 \\\n    --batch_size auto \\\n    --log_samples \\\n    --output_path \"$OUTDIR/\" 2>&1 | tail -20 | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  echo \"EVAL $ARCH pure DONE $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\ndone\n\necho \"=== ALL DONE $(date) ===\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\nSCRIPT\nchmod +x /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion/run_pure_v3.sh\necho \"Script written\"","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":750,"cache_read_input_tokens":113788,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":750},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f91c7e3f-c021-4ec0-8958-c2b588a62e76","uuid":"1907d29b-6981-4cf3-b2f0-d51e91ab1de1"}

But looking at the error from the previous GLA download attempt (the very first one), it was using httpx and received 200MB before the connection dropped. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01PdLuvYs9mMNDhSscShj3Tp","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_011pPqGy1rEna8vW4yi89HuD","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westb-48145","command":"cat > /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion/run_pure_v4.sh << 'SCRIPT'\n#!/bin/bash\nset -eo pipefail\nsource /etc/network_turbo\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\nexport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\nexport HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1\nexport REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\nexport SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\n\nMODELS=\"/root/entrainment_gated_ft/models\"\nRESULTS=\"/root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion\"\nLOG=\"$RESULTS/pure_v4.log\"\nFILES=\"config.json generation_config.json tokenizer.json tokenizer_config.json special_tokens_map.json model.safetensors\"\n\necho \"=== PURE V4 START $(date) ===\" | tee \"$LOG\"\n\n# Download function using wget with non-Xet URL\ndownload_model() {\n  local REPO_NAME=\"$1\"\n  local LOCAL=\"$2\"\n  local ARCH=\"$3\"\n  \n  if [ -f \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\" ]; then\n    SIZE=$(stat -c%s \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\")\n    if [ \"$SIZE\" -gt 100000000 ]; then\n      echo \"$ARCH: exists ($SIZE bytes), skip\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n      return 0\n    fi\n  fi\n  \n  mkdir -p \"$LOCAL\"\n  rm -f \"$LOCAL/.cache\" -rf\n  \n  echo \"DOWNLOAD $ARCH START $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  for f in $FILES; do\n    if [ -f \"$LOCAL/$f\" ] && [ \"$f\" != \"model.safetensors\" ]; then\n      echo \"  $f exists, skip\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n      continue\n    fi\n    echo \"  Downloading $f...\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n    wget --timeout=600 --tries=3 --continue \\\n      \"https://huggingface.co/m-a-p/${REPO_NAME}/resolve/main/${f}?not-for-hierarchical-xet=true\" \\\n      -O \"$LOCAL/$f\" 2>&1 | grep -E '(saved|error|ERROR|failed|100%)' | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n    echo \"  $f done\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  done\n  \n  if [ -f \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\" ]; then\n    SIZE=$(stat -c%s \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\")\n    echo \"DOWNLOAD $ARCH DONE: $SIZE bytes $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  else\n    echo \"DOWNLOAD $ARCH FAILED $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n    return 1\n  fi\n}\n\n# GLA already done\necho \"GLA: checking...\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\nif [ -f \"$MODELS/340M-20B-GLA-pure-baseline/model.safetensors\" ]; then\n  SIZE=$(stat -c%s \"$MODELS/340M-20B-GLA-pure-baseline/model.safetensors\")\n  echo \"GLA: OK ($SIZE bytes)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\nfi\n\n# Download DeltaNet and HGRN\ndownload_model \"340M-20B-DeltaNet-pure\" \"$MODELS/340M-20B-DeltaNet-pure\" \"DeltaNet\"\ndownload_model \"340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline\" \"$MODELS/340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline\" \"HGRN\"\n\n# ---- EVALUATE ALL 3 ----\necho \"=== EVAL START $(date) ===\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n\ndeclare -A DIRS\nDIRS[GLA]=\"$MODELS/340M-20B-GLA-pure-baseline\"\nDIRS[DeltaNet]=\"$MODELS/340M-20B-DeltaNet-pure\"\nDIRS[HGRN]=\"$MODELS/340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline\"\n\nfor ARCH in GLA DeltaNet HGRN; do\n  LOCAL=\"${DIRS[$ARCH]}\"\n  OUTDIR=\"$RESULTS/${ARCH}_pure\"\n  if [ ! -f \"$LOCAL/model.safetensors\" ]; then\n    echo \"SKIP EVAL $ARCH: model missing\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n    continue\n  fi\n  echo \"EVAL $ARCH pure START $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  python -m lm_eval \\\n    --model hf \\\n    --model_args pretrained=$LOCAL,trust_remote_code=True \\\n    --tasks lambada_openai,arc_easy \\\n    --device cuda:0 \\\n    --batch_size auto \\\n    --log_samples \\\n    --output_path \"$OUTDIR/\" 2>&1 | tail -20 | tee -a \"$LOG\"\n  echo \"EVAL $ARCH pure DONE $(date)\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\ndone\n\necho \"=== ALL DONE $(date) ===\" | tee -a \"$LOG\"\nSCRIPT\nchmod +x /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion/run_pure_v4.sh\necho \"OK\"","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":659,"cache_read_input_tokens":120994,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":659},"output_tokens":61,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f91c7e3f-c021-4ec0-8958-c2b588a62e76","uuid":"0037e454-2dff-4170-8789-29442474823c"}
